> **2026-06-12 - NEW OPEN (raised by ORCH-1121 tester, NOT this ORCH's scope) - brand-profile false-empty-flash protection leans on the route auth-spinner, not SECTION E itself.** ORCH-1121 wired the business brand-profile "Recent events" section to a live query and proved the empty card renders only on a settled+non-error+zero query. The tester flagged that the no-flash guarantee on cold load is currently provided by the brand route's auth-spinner gate (which holds the screen until auth resolves), NOT by SECTION E's own loading branch — so if any FUTURE caller ever mounts `BrandProfileView` with a brand resolved before auth settles, the false-empty flash could return. Low severity / latent; SECTION E already checks `isLoading`/`isError` so this is belt-and-suspenders hardening, not a live defect. Not caused by and not fixed in ORCH-1121. Evidence: `Mingla_Artifacts/reports/TEST_ORCH-1121_BRAND_PROFILE_REDESIGN.md`.

> **2026-05-19 — ORCH-0877 [Event end-time display + midnight-crossing single-day authoring] (Path B) CLOSED PASS Grade A.** Investigation by Claude `mingla-forensics` 2026-05-17 returned `root cause proven` — canonical client formatter `formatSingleDateLine(date, doorsOpen)` had no `endsAt` parameter and was used by 18+ sites across consumer iOS/Android, buyer-anon web, business iOS/Android, and email templates; same flaw mirrored on server side at `formatEventDateLine(startAtIso, timezone)` with no `endAtIso`; consumer-feed at `discover-merged-events/index.ts:437-438` explicitly set `endsAtLocal: null`. NOT 18 bugs — ONE structural design choice with 18 symptoms. S-2 authoring root cause: iOS/Android `<DateTimePicker mode="time">` with `minimumDate = doorsOpen + 1 min` at `CreatorStep2When.tsx:352-359` blocked endsAt < doorsOpen on mobile; Web HTML5 picker had no constraint but the model layer dropped the next-day date anyway. DB plumbing was clean end-to-end: `event_dates.end_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL` + CHECK `end_at > start_at` (no same-day rule); both public views project `master_end_at`; publish RPC at `20260604000001_orch_0824_publish_rpc.sql:290-294` already wraps midnight. **Two bonus discoveries also proven in-scope:** (a) Constitution #9 violation in ticket-confirmation ICS where every Mingla ticket ever issued attached a calendar with a fabricated 3-hour end time (`buildCalendarLinks` received `endAtIso: null`); (b) ORCH-0850 hidden flaw — `computeMasterEndAtUtc` reconstructs end-instant from `event.date + event.endsAt`, returning ~20 hours BEFORE start for cross-midnight events and silently misclassifying them as past via lifecycle math (3 production events affected). Same code change fixes both. **Path B scope expansion proven via operator pushback during SPEC:** assumed "edit-and-extend post-fix" flow DOES NOT exist today — `EditPublishedScreen.tsx` mounts `CreatorStep2When` for When but the save path `updateLiveEventFields` writes to Zustand only with NO server-side RPC for `event_dates.end_at`. ORCH-0704 v2 spec at `SPEC_ORCH-0704_FULL_EDIT_AFTER_PUBLISH_v2.md:1303` explicitly says "Database write path is out of scope." Six-field evidence complete on all three root causes. Confidence `probable` with live-fire blocker honestly named (no sim/dev-build in session at INVESTIGATE time; operator promoted to `proven` in <60s via buyer-web tap + mobile-sim authoring tap). Orchestrator REVIEW: APPROVED. **Pipeline complete:** SPEC v2 → IMPLEMENT (45 files) → TEST (31 adversarial + 23 happy-path tests = 54 cases + 4 hotfix = 58 total) → CONDITIONAL PASS → CLOSE PR #136 → operator OTA + smoke → P0 hotfix PR #138 → operator post-OTA smoke 4/4 PASS confirmed → close-paperwork artifact sync (this entry). Verdict PASS P0:0 P1:1 (anon-grant systemic, deferred ORCH-0878) P2:0 P3:1 P4:3 at `reports/QA_ORCH-0877_EVENT_END_TIME_DISPLAY_AND_MIDNIGHT_CROSSING_REPORT.md`. New invariants flipped DRAFT → ACTIVE: I-PROPOSED-EVENT-END-AT-RENDERED-EVERYWHERE, I-PROPOSED-EVENT-AUTHORING-SUPPORTS-CROSS-MIDNIGHT, I-PROPOSED-EVENT-END-AT-FORMATTER-CENTRALIZED. Investigation: `Mingla_Artifacts/reports/INVESTIGATION_ORCH-0877_EVENT_END_TIME_DISPLAY_AND_MIDNIGHT_CROSSING.md`. Spec: `Mingla_Artifacts/specs/SPEC_ORCH-0877_EVENT_END_TIME_DISPLAY_AND_MIDNIGHT_CROSSING.md`. Followups queued: **ORCH-0878 [anon-grant-tightening on business-side patch RPCs]** (single migration with REVOKE for both `business_patch_event_when` + `business_patch_event_taxonomy` + strict-grep CI gate).

> **2026-05-06 (D-B2-23 SDK tech-spike RETURNED + REVIEW APPROVED) — ✅ Spike caught a critical mistake before SPEC writing.** Spike report [`reports/SPIKE_CYCLE_B2_STRIPE_CONNECT_SDK.md`](reports/SPIKE_CYCLE_B2_STRIPE_CONNECT_SDK.md) — 24 sources cited (12 official Stripe docs + 6 GitHub/npm + 6 Expo/community/codebase); H confidence on verdict + 5 gotchas + risk surfaces; no live POC built (research conclusive). **REVIEW APPROVED:** all 10 review checks PASS. **Headline finding:** the original D-B2-23 framing (wrap `@stripe/connect-js` in `react-native-webview` ourselves) is **EXPLICITLY PROHIBITED** by Stripe's published docs — verbatim from [docs.stripe.com/connect/get-started-connect-embedded-components](https://docs.stripe.com/connect/get-started-connect-embedded-components): *"You can't use Connect embedded components in embedded web views inside mobile or desktop applications."* Building this would have violated Stripe's prescribed integration model with technical (Stripe could disable the integration) + legal (Connect Platform Agreement) consequences. **Path A revised:** Stripe ships native React Native `<ConnectAccountOnboarding>` component in `@stripe/stripe-react-native@≥0.59.0` (released 2026-02-20 in **private preview**). This is Stripe's official prescribed path for our exact use case. The SDK uses `react-native-webview` internally as Stripe's chosen implementation detail, but Stripe owns all the WebView complexity — Mingla writes plain RN component code. **5 material gotchas to encode in B2a SPEC:** G-1 (S0) operator must request private preview access from Stripe — calendar gate; G-2 (S1) SDK upgrade `0.50.3` → `0.65.0` (15 minor versions; 2 known breaking change points at v0.60.0 + v0.62.0); G-3 (S1) `react-native-webview` peer dep requires NATIVE REBUILD (NOT OTA-eligible) — bundle with Cycle 16b's pending native window per `feedback_eas_update_no_web`; G-4 (S0) Expo Web parallel SDK path required (`@stripe/connect-js` + `@stripe/react-connect-js` for web target since native components don't render under React-Native-Web); G-5 (S1) pin SDK to exact version + CI grep gate against silent upgrades. **Risk surface verdicts:** 10 of 12 ✅ green (R-A1..R-A2 native rendering / R-A4..R-A8 asset loading + camera + Plaid + onExit + backgrounding / R-A9..R-A12 store policies + ToS + resume + branding); 2 ⚠️ Expo Web parallel path (R-A1 Expo Web + R-A3 bundle compatibility — both folded into G-4). **Stripe ToS verified:** [Connect Account Agreement](https://stripe.com/legal/connect-account) contains zero embedding-restriction clauses; the "no WebView wrap" prohibition lives only in technical docs. Path A revised is contractually + technically permitted. **Plan B (custom REST form, 3-4 weeks) and Plan C (hosted redirect, violates R2)** sized for completeness — neither recommended unless Path A blocked. **6 side-issue discoveries (DISC-S1..S6):** S1 SDK refresh is shared prerequisite for B2a + B3 + B4 (fold into B2a §A.1); S2 bundle Cycle 16b native rebuild with B2a (saves App Store review); S3 propose `I-PROPOSED-J` invariant codifying "Stripe Embedded Components via official SDK only — no DIY WebView wrap"; S4 B2a tester dispatch needs iOS + Android + Expo Web smoke gates (test scope doubles); S5 operator should dispatch Stripe access request TODAY (parallel calendar gate; >2 week delay slips B2a); S6 documentation-vs-legal split applies to future Stripe integrations (B3 PaymentElement, B4 Terminal). **Verdict shape:** ⚠️ Path A viable WITH 5 gotchas. **What this saved:** ~5-7 days of B2a rework if SPEC had committed to the original WebView-wrap approach + potential Stripe Connect Platform Agreement violation. > **2026-05-06 (LATEST — ORCH-0734 dispatched) — 🔬 ROOT CAUSE PROVEN AFTER 13 PASSES → ORCH-0734 RLS-RETURNING-OWNER-GAP audit DISPATCHED.** Brand-create + brand-delete bugs share a single structural root cause now formally classified RC-0728 in [ROOT_CAUSE_REGISTER.md](ROOT_CAUSE_REGISTER.md). PASS-13 evidence chain: H39 (DISABLE/ENABLE RLS proves RLS is the denier) → H40 (JWT decode proves JWT sub === user.id, JWT not the bug) → H41 (pg_policies enumeration proves only 5 policies on brands; no owner-SELECT for fresh row + no SELECT admits soft-deleted row) → H42 (pure INSERT *without* RETURNING succeeded under simulated auth — definitive: SELECT-for-RETURNING is the denier). The bug class: any supabase-js `.insert/.update/.delete(...).select()` call (which sets `Prefer: return=representation`) makes Postgres evaluate SELECT policies on the post-mutation row as part of RETURNING; if no SELECT policy admits that row state, the entire mutation rolls back with 42501 — even though WITH CHECK passed. **ORCH-0734 dispatched (mingla-business scope only; app-mobile excluded per operator directive):** `prompts/INVESTIGATION_ORCH_0734_RLS_RETURNING_OWNER_GAP_AUDIT.md` (PRIVATE_PROMPT_NOT_VERSIONED: `prompts/INVESTIGATION_ORCH_0734_RLS_RETURNING_OWNER_GAP_AUDIT.md`) — comprehensive audit of every authenticated INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE policy on every mingla-business B1+ table for matching post-mutation SELECT-policy coverage, plus binding fix spec covering all gaps + new invariant I-PROPOSED-H + CI gate + permanent skill memory. If worker dies after the mark but before completing rows, those rows stuck in 'running' status forever. Heartbeat-based pg_cron recovery handles parent level but not row level. **Fix:** worker pickup SELECT in step 4 must be `WHERE status='pending' OR (status='running' AND started_at < now() - interval '5 minutes')` to self-heal stale row-level state. ~5 lines implementor amendment to §3.1. **Gap 2 (S1-high) RLS policy broken** — SPEC §2 migration step 4: `auth.uid() IN (SELECT id FROM admin_users WHERE status='active')`. **VERIFIED VIA LIVE SQL PROBE: `admin_users.id ≠ auth.users.id`.** Live evidence: `seth@usemingla.com` has `admin_users.id=4c33...` but `auth.users.id=63835860...`; `ids_match=false` for all 2 admin users. Existing edge fn correctly matches admin_users by EMAIL (line 309-314 of `run-place-intelligence-trial/index.ts`). The SPEC's RLS policy as written would BLOCK all admin reads/writes. **Fix:** RLS policy must JOIN through email: `EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM auth.users u JOIN admin_users au ON au.email=u.email WHERE u.id=auth.uid() AND au.status='active')`. ~10 lines implementor amendment to §2 migration. **Both gaps mechanical** (no architecture rework); implementor dispatch can proceed with explicit amendment notes. **Path forward (operator decides):** (a) accept SPEC + dispatch implementor with the 2 gap-fix notes baked into the IMPL prompt; (b) send back to forensics for SPEC v2 patch (formal but slower; +30 min cycle time). Recommend (a) because both gaps are mechanical and the rest of SPEC is rigorous. **Active queue post-this-update:** ORCH-0737 implementor dispatch authoring (orchestrator next); Cycle 17e-A close-pending; ORCH-0736 + ORCH-0738 queued ~1 week each; 2026-05-19 backup snapshot DROP reminder for `_archive_orch_0734_signal_anchors`.

It does NOT handle **legacy-row + legacy-row → both rename to same canonical** case (e.g., both `nature_views` AND `picnic_park` exist for `amusement_park`; both rename to `nature` — UPDATE collapses them onto the same PK during second row's rename). Handles all 3 collision cases naturally: (1) legacy + canonical = INSERT skipped via ON CONFLICT, legacy DELETE removes; (2) legacy + legacy → same canonical = first INSERT lands, second skipped, both legacies DELETEd; (3) no collision = INSERT lands, legacy DELETEd. Production unaffected (same data outcome). NEW dispatch `prompts/IMPLEMENTOR_ORCH-0727_REWORK_SLUG_RENAME_OPTION_B.md` (PRIVATE_PROMPT_NOT_VERSIONED: `prompts/IMPLEMENTOR_ORCH-0727_REWORK_SLUG_RENAME_OPTION_B.md`).
